- **ast-ceo**: CEO (Elon Musk)｜跨职能、公司级或高不确定性任务的总协调者。仅当任务跨两个以上 C-suite 领域、需要在资源/预算/范围/时间之间取舍，或多个专业结论冲突需要综合时调用；用户明确要求 CEO 或公司级决策也算。典型信号：公司级、跨职能、资源取舍、路线图、最终决策。 不适用：只涉及单一专业领域的判断、已由对应 C-suite 认领的执行细节、关键输入缺失就要拍板，或需要独立证据复核而非综合决策。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-ceo.md))
- **ast-cfo**: CFO (Warren Buffett)｜需要预算与滚动预测、单位经济与定价证据、资本配置取舍、财务控制或成本优化时调用。典型信号：预算、定价、单位经济、成本、现金流、融资。 不适用：合同条款与合规判断、销售与客户关系推进、产品范围取舍，或需要实际付款、过账与税务申报操作。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cfo.md))
- **ast-clo**: CLO (Alan Dershowitz)｜需要合同与协议风险评审、隐私与跨境合规检查、许可与知识产权问题识别、监管政策适用判断时调用。典型信号：合同、隐私、合规、许可、知识产权、监管。 不适用：需要特定司法辖区的正式法律意见或诉讼代理、商业与财务取舍、技术安全实现，或交易结构与事实尚未明确。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-clo.md))
- **ast-cmo**: CMO (David Ogilvy)｜需要市场定位与品牌策略、渠道增长实验、付费投放或 SEO 方案、获客测量与归因设计时调用。典型信号：品牌、获客、投放、广告、SEO、定位、归因。 不适用：内容选题、制作与渠道适配执行、产品功能与承诺取舍、定价与预算决策，或缺少受众与转化证据的品牌空转。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cmo.md))
- **ast-coo**: COO (Andy Grove)｜需要运营计划与执行节奏、责任与依赖映射、跨部门流程与 SOP 设计、产能规划或异常恢复安排时调用。典型信号：运营、SOP、流程、排期、产能、跨部门。 不适用：产品范围取舍、技术架构选型、财务预算与定价决策，或需要独立证据复核与放行门判定。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-coo.md))
- **ast-cpo**: CPO (Steve Jobs)｜需要产品方向取舍、用户问题定义与需求澄清、体验与可访问性评审、优先级排序或可验证需求拆解时调用。典型信号：产品、需求、用户、体验、优先级、PRD、功能范围。 不适用：技术可行性与架构选型、获客渠道与投放执行、跨部门交付排期，或用户证据已充分只需界面与信息架构设计。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cpo.md))
- **ast-cqo**: CQO (Jim Simons)｜需要量化假设设计、回测与复现、模型评估与独立复核、组合风险控制或实验预登记时调用。典型信号：量化、回测、策略、因子、A股、选股、组合风险。 不适用：需要资本配置与预算决策、实盘下单或交易执行、经营现金流与利润预测，或问题缺少可复现数据与量化口径。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cqo.md))
- **ast-cro**: CRO (Richard Feynman)｜需要研究问题设计、一手证据获取与核验、竞品或市场情报扫描、多源证据综合与不确定性说明时调用。典型信号：研究、证据、竞品、调研、核验、市场情报。 不适用：需要财务、法律、产品或营销的最终结论、纯执行性的内容与投放工作、需要登录账号或联系第三方，或问题已有明确实验可直接验证。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cro.md))
- **ast-cso**: CSO (Michael Dell)｜需要客户发现与资格审查、赢单策略、方案与投标叙事、销售管道分析或客户成功交接设计时调用。典型信号：销售、客户、商机、赢单、投标、管道。 不适用：合同条款与合规判断、定价与单位经济测算、大规模需求生成与品牌投放，或尚无合格商机就要承诺交付与收入。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cso.md))
- **ast-cto**: CTO (Jensen Huang)｜需要技术战略选择、架构边界划分、接口/API 设计评审、技术选型对比、平台成本评估、系统可靠性设计或故障复盘时调用。典型信号：架构、技术选型、API/接口、性能、可靠性、故障复盘、系统设计。 不适用：实现层面的编码与集成、纯产品取舍、财务与法务判断、数据平台治理；非技术问题不得调用 CTO，也不要把 CTO 当作兜底角色。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-cto.md))
- **ast-detector-writer**: Use when drafting a new tree-sitter-based detect.ast rule for guideline-checker (Python/TS/JS/JSX/TSX), including its proving fixture. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: user wants a new AST detector for a naming convention. user: "add a detector that flags snake_case React component files" assistant: "I'll use the ast-detector-writer agent to draft the tree-sitter query and a fixture proving it fires on a violation and not on a compliant file."&lt;/example&gt; ([chrysa/guideline-checker](https://github.com/chrysa/guideline-checker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ast-detector-writer.md))
- **ast-governor**: Governor｜重大结论与完成声明的独立证据复核者。需要独立复核重大结论、发布、安全、资金、法律或完成声明，并给出放行门决定或风险升级时调用。典型信号：复核、放行、审计、风险升级、完成声明。 不适用：需要设计与实现交付物、替领域负责人作专业判断、替 CEO 作资源取舍，或声明方无法提供可复核证据。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-governor.md))

- **ast-grep**: Use this agent when users need to perform AST-aware code searches, write structural code patterns, create ast-grep rules, or perform code transformations using syntax tree matching. This agent helps write correct ast-grep patterns and YAML rules for finding code structures like "functions without error handling" or "React components using specific hooks." The agent who would have caught the round-9 MultiEdit matcher gap structurally — by reading the grammar, not by running the corpus. - **ast-pe**: PE (Linus Torvalds)｜已批准的设计需要跨模块实现、集成、测试、代码评审、缺陷修复或可回退交付时调用。典型信号：实现、编码、集成、测试、重构、缺陷修复、交付。 不适用：设计尚未批准、需要产品或优先级取舍、架构边界存在争议，或只需窄领域专家给出专项建议而不承担跨模块实现。 ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/blob/HEAD/plugins/agi-super-team-claudecode/.claude/agents/ast-pe.md))
- **astack-comment-judge**: Review scoped comments against the keep clauses. Delete comments that fail them and report application-code changes as MUST KILL targets. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-comment-judge.md))
- **astack-delegate**: Delegate subagent for code and helper tasks during a `/astack-mode` run. Use for delegates, not as the runner for `/astack-mode` itself. Spawn a fresh `astack-delegate` for each new task. Resume one only in the strict cases that the mode's Subagents rules name. Read the full mode skill and the principles index before any work. A `general-purpose` substitute skips that read and drifts. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-delegate.md))
- **astack-explorer**: Read repo facts, review one diff axis, or outline an interface without changes. Used by astack-grilling, astack-code-review, astack-improve-codebase-architecture, and design-it-twice. - **astack-postgres-reviewer**: Use for a requested read-only PostgreSQL review of a named diff. Reports verified behavior defects and proof limits. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-postgres-reviewer.md))
- **astack-researcher**: Research one question for astack-research or wayfinder. Write one cited Markdown file at the path or branch in the brief. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-researcher.md))
- **astack-typescript-reviewer**: Use for a requested read-only TypeScript and TSX review of a named diff. Reports verified behavior defects and proof limits. ([vahaah/astack](https://github.com/vahaah/astack/blob/HEAD/dist/cursor/astack/agents/astack-typescript-reviewer.md))
- **astera-reviewer**: Astera-specific read-only code review for Phase C close. Verifies Konsist green, application APIs use Result&lt;T,E&gt;, no hardcoded versions, i18n parity, ADR conventions, and that the wave did not touch off-limits paths. Reports violations; does not fix. ([AstarWorks/Astera](https://github.com/AstarWorks/Astera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/astera-reviewer.md))
- **astera-worker**: Phase B implementation worker for Astera waves / cleanup passes. Implements one scoped territory (files, tests, YAML) handed over by the Opus orchestrator. Does not wire platform/Astera*Plugin.kt, write ADRs, run gradle, or touch git — it reports wiring needs back instead. - **asteroid-belt**: &gt; "An asteroid is not about size. It's about velocity and density. - **astra**: General-purpose subagent on OpenAI GPT-6 Astra. Use when a task benefits from a non-Anthropic model: independent verification, cross-model second opinions, adversarial review of Claude-authored work, or bounded implementation and debugging. Full tool access; finishes the task it is handed and reports with evidence. ([sjawhar/dotfiles](https://github.com/sjawhar/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/omp/agents/astra.md) · ★5)
- **astra**: GPT-6 Astra for consequential architecture, planning, research synthesis, and adversarial review. The code shows what the app means to do. You are here to find out what it actually does. ([jgoetzmann/polish-website](https://github.com/jgoetzmann/polish-website/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker.md))
- **attacker-ai**: Red Team. Loyihadagi AI funksiyalarini aldash (prompt injection) va AI kalitlarini suiiste'mol qilish yo'llarini kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-ai.md))
- **attacker-apk**: Red Team. APKni ochib o'qiydigan hujumchi sifatida ilova ichidagi sirlar, ochiq komponentlar va himoyasiz aloqani qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-apk.md))
- **attacker-cost**: Red Team. Xarajat hujumchisi sifatida limitsiz so'rovlar orqali API va hosting hisobini oshirish yo'llarini kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-cost.md))
- **attacker-deploy**: Red Team. Infratuzilma hujumchisi sifatida deploy, baza qoidalari, Docker va hosting sozlamalaridagi ochiq joylarni qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-deploy.md))
- **attacker-fraudster**: Red Team. Biznes-mantiq firibgari sifatida to'lov, balans, Stars, promo va referal oqimlaridagi mantiqiy teshiklarni qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-fraudster.md))
- **attacker-insider**: Red Team. Sobiq admin yoki xodim sifatida eski kirish huquqlari va nazoratsiz admin amallarini kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-insider.md))
- **attacker-outsider**: Red Team. Tashqaridan, hisobsiz anonim hujumchi sifatida kodni o'qib, ochiq eshiklarni topadi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-outsider.md))
- **attacker-phisher**: Red Team. Soxtalashtiruvchi sifatida loyiha nomidan foydalanuvchilarni aldashga yo'l ochadigan joylarni (redirect, deep link, webhook, xabarlar) kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-phisher.md))
- **attacker-scraper**: Red Team. Ma'lumot yig'uvchi sifatida foydalanuvchilar ma'lumotini ommaviy tortib olish va shaxsiy ma'lumot sizishi yo'llarini kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-scraper.md))
- **attacker-supply**: Red Team. Ta'minot zanjiri hujumchisi sifatida bog'liqliklar, mavjud bo'lmagan paketlar va CI sozlamalaridagi xavflarni qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-supply.md))
- **attacker-takeover**: Red Team. Akkaunt o'g'risi sifatida login, parol tiklash, OTP va sessiya mantiqidagi zaifliklarni kodda qidiradi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-takeover.md))
- **attacker-token**: Red Team. Sizib chiqqan token yoki kalit hujumchi qo'liga tushsa nima qila olishini baholaydi. Faqat o'qiydi. ([RakhimovUK/VibeGuard](https://github.com/RakhimovUK/VibeGuard/blob/HEAD/agents/attacker-token.md))

- **attention-analyst**: LLM 内部の mechanistic interpretability 専門エージェント。logit lens / attention pattern / activation patching / path patching / probing classifier / SAE feature lookup を TransformerLens (≤7B) や nnsight (&gt;7B) で実行し、causal な機構解釈を提供する。 ml-engineer は LLM を black-box として扱うが、こちらは glass-box として扱う。 Use when: focus_area=attention のプロジェクトで Phase 5/6 に内部解析を行うとき、 または「なぜモデル X は Y で失敗するのか?」のような mechanistic な問いに答えるとき。 &lt;example&gt; Context: SFT 後のモデルで TriviaQA 性能が落ちた。何が壊れたか調べたい。 user: "After SFT on math, TriviaQA dropped 8 points. - **Audit - Code, Infra, Refactor, Security**: Audits one repository for code quality, infrastructure, architecture, and security. Produces documents only, unless you ask for researched fix proposals or remediation — then it drives the fixes through the feature pipeline. To compare two revisions or checkouts, use Audit - Delta instead. - **Audit - Delta**: Audits two or more revisions or checkouts of the same product independently, then reconciles each pair into a delta report of what changed — resolved, improved, unchanged, transformed, and new — keeping genuine regressions separate from pre-existing findings only the newer audit raised. Produces documents only, unless you ask for researched fix proposals or remediation. ([threnjen/thren-agentic-workflows](https://github.com/threnjen/thren-agentic-workflows/blob/HEAD/source_of_truth/agents/delta-auditor.agent.md) · ★2)
